- 2026-10-02 PROBE P2 (/skyprobe secgrid) did NOT run: our probe bug - java.lang.IllegalAccessError, ProbeWin called the protected CustomUIPage.sendUpdate from a non-subclass (javassist compiles it, the JVM refuses it at run time; the bare-JVM harness only loaded the class). Fix: SkyyUiProbe 0.3.1 (protected engine members only from the page subclass on 'this'; the harness now executes the open paths). LESSON for every build: call protected engine methods only from a subclass on this, and make harnesses run the code path. P1 log: the server sent window 1 with the 3 probe items, the client drew nothing next to the page.
